### [119] [An Existence Proof for Neural Language Models That Can Explain Garden-Path Effects via Surprisal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18293)
*Ryo Yoshida,Shinnosuke Isono,Taiga Someya,Yohei Oseki,Tatsuki Kuribayashi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过微调语言模型在花园路径句上，证明神经语言模型能够通过惊奇度理论同时解释花园路径效应和自然阅读时间


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经语言模型在自然语料上能通过惊奇度预测人类处理难度，但在需要句法消歧的花园路径句上严重低估处理难度，导致有人认为这类句子的处理难度不能简化为惊奇度。本文旨在探究是否真的无法构建能通过惊奇度解释花园路径效应的神经语言模型。

Method: 不直接使用现成的神经语言模型，而是对这些模型在花园路径句上进行微调，以更好地对齐基于惊奇度的阅读时间估计与实际人类阅读时间。评估微调后的模型是否过拟合，以及能否捕捉人类在保留花园路径项上的阅读减速。

Result: 微调后的语言模型没有过拟合，成功捕捉了人类在保留花园路径项上的阅读减速；甚至提高了对自然语料中人类阅读时间的预测能力，同时保留了其通用语言模型能力。

Conclusion: 结果证明了存在一种神经语言模型能够通过惊奇度同时解释花园路径效应和自然阅读时间，但也提出了一个理论问题：什么样的证据才能真正证伪惊奇度理论？

Abstract: Surprisal theory hypothesizes that the difficulty of human sentence processing increases linearly with surprisal, the negative log-probability of a word given its context. Computational psycholinguistics has tested this hypothesis using language models (LMs) as proxies for human prediction. While surprisal derived from recent neural LMs generally captures human processing difficulty on naturalistic corpora that predominantly consist of simple sentences, it severely underestimates processing difficulty on sentences that require syntactic disambiguation (garden-path effects). This leads to the claim that the processing difficulty of such sentences cannot be reduced to surprisal, although it remains possible that neural LMs simply differ from humans in next-word prediction. In this paper, we investigate whether it is truly impossible to construct a neural LM that can explain garden-path effects via surprisal. Specifically, instead of evaluating off-the-shelf neural LMs, we fine-tune these LMs on garden-path sentences so as to better align surprisal-based reading-time estimates with actual human reading times. Our results show that fine-tuned LMs do not overfit and successfully capture human reading slowdowns on held-out garden-path items; they even improve predictive power for human reading times on naturalistic corpora and preserve their general LM capabilities. These results provide an existence proof for a neural LM that can explain both garden-path effects and naturalistic reading times via surprisal, but also raise a theoretical question: what kind of evidence can truly falsify surprisal theory?

</details>


### [120] [Exploring Concreteness Through a Figurative Lens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18296)
*Saptarshi Ghosh,Tianyu Jiang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文分析了LLM如何内部理解具体性概念，发现模型在早期层区分字面和比喻用法，在中后期层将具体性压缩为一维方向，该方向可用于比喻语言分类和无训练生成控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态具体性评分在NLP中广泛使用，但词语的具体性会随上下文变化，特别是在比喻语言中。虽然这种变化可以从上下文中看出，但LLM如何在内部理解具体性仍不清楚。需要分析LLM隐藏表示如何区分同一名词的字面和比喻用法，以及具体性在表示空间中如何组织。

Method: 对四个模型家族的LLM隐藏表示进行分层和几何分析：1）检查模型如何区分同一名词的字面和比喻用法；2）分析具体性在表示空间中的组织方式；3）发现具体性被压缩为一维方向；4）验证该几何结构的实用性。

Result: 1）LLM在早期层分离字面和比喻用法；2）中后期层将具体性压缩为一维方向，该方向在不同模型中保持一致；3）该具体性方向支持高效的比喻语言分类；4）可实现无需训练的控制生成，使改写更偏向字面或比喻表达。

Conclusion: LLM内部形成了对具体性的结构化表示，在表示空间中存在一致的具体性方向。这一几何结构具有实际应用价值，可用于比喻语言分类和生成控制，为理解LLM如何编码语义属性提供了新见解。

Abstract: Static concreteness ratings are widely used in NLP, yet a word's concreteness can shift with context, especially in figurative language such as metaphor, where common concrete nouns can take abstract interpretations. While such shifts are evident from context, it remains unclear how LLMs understand concreteness internally. We conduct a layer-wise and geometric analysis of LLM hidden representations across four model families, examining how models distinguish literal vs figurative uses of the same noun and how concreteness is organized in representation space. We find that LLMs separate literal and figurative usage in early layers, and that mid-to-late layers compress concreteness into a one-dimensional direction that is consistent across models. Finally, we show that this geometric structure is practically useful: a single concreteness direction supports efficient figurative-language classification and enables training-free steering of generation toward more literal or more figurative rewrites.

</details>


### [121] [Reasoning Models Know What's Important, and Encode It in Their Activations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18307)
*Yaniv Nikankin,Martin Tutek,Tomer Ashuach,Jonathan Rosenfeld,Yonatan Belinkov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现语言模型在推理过程中，模型激活比推理链的token包含更多关于步骤重要性的信息，表明分析模型内部表示比表面特征更能揭示推理机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型通过生成包含多个步骤的推理链来解决复杂任务，但不同步骤的重要性不同。理解哪些步骤重要以及为什么重要，对于理解模型如何处理推理过程至关重要。研究者希望探索这个问题应该通过模型内部表示还是推理链的token本身来研究。

Method: 通过训练探针（probes）在模型激活上预测步骤重要性，研究模型是否编码了步骤重要性的内部表示。比较模型激活与token在识别重要推理步骤方面的信息含量，并分析重要性表示是否分布在多个层中，以及与表面特征（如相对位置、长度）的相关性。

Result: 1) 模型激活比token包含更多识别重要推理步骤的信息；2) 模型在生成后续步骤之前就编码了步骤重要性的内部表示；3) 这种重要性表示在不同模型间具有通用性；4) 重要性表示分布在多个层中；5) 重要性表示与表面特征（如步骤的相对位置或长度）不相关。

Conclusion: 分析模型激活可以揭示表面方法根本无法发现的推理方面，表明推理分析应该深入模型内部表示，而不仅仅是关注推理链的表面特征。这为理解语言模型的推理机制提供了新的视角和方法。

Abstract: Language models often solve complex tasks by generating long reasoning chains, consisting of many steps with varying importance. While some steps are crucial for generating the final answer, others are removable. Determining which steps matter most, and why, remains an open question central to understanding how models process reasoning. We investigate if this question is best approached through model internals or through tokens of the reasoning chain itself. We find that model activations contain more information than tokens for identifying important reasoning steps. Crucially, by training probes on model activations to predict importance, we show that models encode an internal representation of step importance, even prior to the generation of subsequent steps. This internal representation of importance generalizes across models, is distributed across layers, and does not correlate with surface-level features, such as a step's relative position or its length. Our findings suggest that analyzing activations can reveal aspects of reasoning that surface-level approaches fundamentally miss, indicating that reasoning analyses should look into model internals.

</details>


### [122] [On the Importance and Evaluation of Narrativity in Natural Language AI Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18311)
*Mateusz Cedro,David Martens*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出将可解释AI（XAI）解释以叙事形式呈现，开发了七个自动指标来量化解释的叙事质量，并提出了问题无关的XAI叙事生成规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前XAI解释方法通常呈现静态特征重要性列表，虽然能显示什么影响了预测，但不能解释为什么发生预测。基于社会科学和语言学的见解，研究者认为XAI解释应以叙事形式呈现，因为叙事通过连续结构、因果机制、语言流畅性和词汇多样性支持人类理解。

Method: 1) 分析叙事解释的四个关键属性：连续结构、因果机制、语言流畅性、词汇多样性；2) 指出标准NLP指标（基于词频或词概率）无法捕捉这些属性；3) 提出七个自动指标来量化解释的叙事质量；4) 在六个数据集上对当前最先进的解释生成方法进行基准测试；5) 提出一套问题无关的XAI叙事生成规则。

Result: 提出的七个自动指标比标准NLP指标更可靠地区分描述性解释和叙事解释。研究展示了如何通过叙事生成规则产生具有更强叙事属性的自然语言XAI解释，使其与语言学和社科文献的发现保持一致。

Conclusion: XAI解释应以叙事形式呈现以增强可理解性，提出的叙事质量指标能有效评估解释质量，而问题无关的XAI叙事生成规则为生成高质量叙事解释提供了实用框架，推动了可解释AI向更人性化、更易理解的方向发展。

Abstract: Explainable AI (XAI) aims to make the behaviour of machine learning models interpretable, yet many explanation methods remain difficult to understand. The integration of Natural Language Generation into XAI aims to deliver explanations in textual form, making them more accessible to practitioners. Current approaches, however, largely yield static lists of feature importances. Although such explanations indicate what influences the prediction, they do not explain why the prediction occurs. In this study, we draw on insights from social sciences and linguistics, and argue that XAI explanations should be presented in the form of narratives. Narrative explanations support human understanding through four defining properties: continuous structure, cause-effect mechanisms, linguistic fluency, and lexical diversity. We show that standard Natural Language Processing (NLP) metrics based solely on token probability or word frequency fail to capture these properties and can be matched or exceeded by tautological text that conveys no explanatory content. To address this issue, we propose seven automatic metrics that quantify the narrative quality of explanations along the four identified dimensions. We benchmark current state-of-the-art explanation generation methods on six datasets and show that the proposed metrics separate descriptive from narrative explanations more reliably than standard NLP metrics. Finally, to further advance the field, we propose a set of problem-agnostic XAI Narrative generation rules for producing natural language XAI explanations, so that the resulting XAI Narratives exhibit stronger narrative properties and align with the findings from the linguistic and social science literature.

</details>


### [123] [Comparing Human and Large Language Model Interpretation of Implicit Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17085)
*Antonio De Santis,Tommaso Bonetti,Andrea Tocchetti,Marco Brambilla*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出隐式信息抽取任务，构建基于LLM的IIE流程从上下文提取关系三元组、验证隐式推理和分析时间关系，发现LLM在隐式信息提取方面覆盖有限且比人类更保守


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐式意义理解是人类交流的重要组成部分，但这一框架可能不适用于与大型语言模型的交互。需要研究LLM在隐式信息提取方面的能力，了解模型与人类在隐式推理上的差异

Method: 提出隐式信息抽取任务，设计基于LLM的IIE流程：从上下文句子中提取关系三元组、验证隐式推理、分析时间关系。在两个数据集上评估两个LLM，并与众包人类判断进行比较

Result: 人类同意大多数模型提取的三元组，但会提出许多补充，表明当前基于LLM的IIE覆盖有限。在社交丰富的语境中，模型比人类更保守于隐式推理；而在简短的事实导向语境中，人类变得更保守

Conclusion: LLM在隐式信息提取方面存在局限性，模型与人类在隐式推理上的保守性差异取决于语境类型。需要改进LLM的隐式信息提取能力以更接近人类的理解水平

Abstract: The interpretation of implicit meanings is an integral aspect of human communication. However, this framework may not transfer to interactions with Large Language Models (LLMs). To investigate this, we introduce the task of Implicit Information Extraction (IIE) and propose an LLM-based IIE pipeline that builds a structured knowledge graph from a context sentence by extracting relational triplets, validating implicit inferences, and analyzing temporal relations. We evaluate two LLMs against crowdsourced human judgments on two datasets. We find that humans agree with most model triplets yet consistently propose many additions, indicating limited coverage in current LLM-based IIE. Moreover, in our experiments, models appear to be more conservative about implicit inferences than humans in socially rich contexts, whereas humans become more conservative in shorter, fact-oriented contexts. Our code is available at https://github.com/Antonio-Dee/IIE_from_LLM.

</details>


### [124] [Beyond Word Boundaries: A Hebrew Coreference Benchmark and an Evaluation Protocol for Morphologically Complex Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17108)
*Refael Shaked Greenfeld,Reut Tsarfaty*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文介绍了KibutzR，这是首个针对现代希伯来语的全面指代消解数据集，解决了形态丰富语言中词边界与指代边界不对齐的问题，并提出了新的评估协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指代消解是NLP基础任务，但现有方法主要针对英语设计，在形态丰富语言中表现不佳，因为词边界与指代边界不对齐，且单个词素可能包含多个指代项。需要专门的数据集和评估方法来处理希伯来语等形态丰富语言。

Method: 1) 创建KibutzR数据集，在词、子词和多词层面标注指代项；2) 提出直接处理词/语素边界差异的评估协议；3) 实验比较当代LLM在希伯来语和英语上的表现差异。

Result: 1) 当代LLM在希伯来语上的表现显著差于英语；2) 在原始未分词文本上性能下降；3) 希伯来语呈现与英语相反的性能趋势：较小的编码器模型表现优于当代解码器模型。

Conclusion: 该研究为希伯来语指代消解提供了新基准和分词感知的评估协议，揭示了形态丰富语言中LLM性能的独特模式，为未来研究其他形态丰富语言提供了参考框架。

Abstract: Coreference Resolution (CR) is a fundamental NLP task critical for long-form tasks as information extraction, summarization, and many business applications. However, CR methods originally designed for English struggle with Morphologically Rich Languages (MRLs), where mention boundaries do not necessarily align with word boundaries, and a single token may consist of multiple anaphors. CR modeling and evaluation protocols standardly assume that, as in English, words and mentions mostly align. However, this assumption breaks down in MRLs, particularly in the context of LLMs' raw-text processing and end-to-end tasks. To assess and address this challenge, we introduce {\em KibutzR}, the first comprehensive CR dataset for Modern Hebrew, an MRL rich with complex words and pronominal clitics. We deliver an annotated dataset that identifies mentions at word, sub-word and multi-word levels, and propose an evaluation protocol that directly addresses word/morpheme boundary discrepancies. Our experiments show that contemporary LLMs perform significantly worse on Hebrew than on English, and that performance degrades on raw unsegmented text. Crucially, we show an inverse performance-trend in Hebrew relative to English, where smaller encoders perform far better than contemporary decoder models, leaving ample space for investigation and improvement. We deliver a new benchmark for Hebrew coreference resolution and a segmentation-aware evaluation protocol to inform future work on other MRLs.

</details>


### [125] [Multilingual Training and Evaluation Resources for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18347)
*Daniela Baiamonte,Elena Fano,Matteo Gabburo,Stefano Simonazzi,Leonardo Rigutini,Andrea Zugarini*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一套针对五种欧洲语言（英、法、德、意、西）的视觉语言模型训练和评估资源，通过再生-翻译范式构建高质量多语言多模态数据集，并证明多语言训练对非英语基准测试有显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉语言模型发展严重依赖英语资源，存在两个主要局限：1）缺乏多语言多模态训练数据集；2）缺少跨语言的全面评估基准。这限制了VLMs在多语言环境中的应用和发展。

Method: 采用再生-翻译范式：1）构建Multi-PixMo训练语料库，通过许可模型再生Pixmo现有数据集（PixMo-Cap、PixMo-AskModelAnything、CoSyn-400k）；2）创建多语言评估基准，通过翻译广泛使用的英语数据集（MMbench、ScienceQA、MME、POPE、AI2D）；3）通过人工标注和定量分析验证资源质量。

Result: 1）资源质量验证显示高标注一致性；2）消融研究表明多语言数据相比仅英语训练对VLMs有显著提升；3）在3个不同模型上的实验证明，使用多语言多模态示例训练VLMs对非英语基准测试一致有益，同时对英语也有正向迁移效果。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了视觉语言模型在多语言资源方面的空白，提出的再生-翻译范式能够生成高质量跨语言资源，实验证明多语言训练对VLMs性能提升具有重要价值，为多语言视觉语言模型的发展提供了重要基础设施。

Abstract: Vision Language Models (VLMs) achieved rapid progress in the recent years. However, despite their growth, VLMs development is heavily grounded on English, leading to two main limitations: (i) the lack of multilingual and multimodal datasets for training, and (ii) the scarcity of comprehensive evaluation benchmarks across languages. In this work, we address these gaps by introducing a new comprehensive suite of resources for VLMs training and evaluation spanning five European languages (English, French, German, Italian, and Spanish). We adopt a regeneration-translation paradigm that produces high-quality cross-lingual resources by combining curated synthetic generation and manual annotation. Specifically, we build Multi-PixMo, a training corpus obtained regenerating examples from Pixmo pre-existing datasets with permissively licensed models: PixMo-Cap, PixMo-AskModelAnything, and CoSyn-400k. On the evaluation side, we construct a set of multilingual benchmarks derived translating widely used English datasets (MMbench, ScienceQA, MME, POPE, AI2D). We assess the quality of these resources through qualitative and quantitative human analyses, measuring inter-annotator agreement. Additionally, we perform ablation studies to demonstrate the impact of multilingual data, with respect to English only, in VLMs training. Experiments, comprising 3 different models show that using multilingual, multimodal examples for training VLMs aids is consistently beneficial on non-English benchmarks, with positive transfer to English as well.

</details>


### [126] [The Consensus Trap: Rescuing Multi-Agent LLMs from Adversarial Majorities via Token-Level Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17139)
*Jiayuan Liu,Shiyi Du,Weihua Du,Mingyu Guo,Vincent Conitzer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 多智能体LLM系统在开放环境中易受上下文污染攻击，现有基于多数投票的响应级聚合在污染智能体形成局部多数时会崩溃。本文提出令牌级轮转协作机制，通过智能体在共享自回归上下文中顺序交错生成，将聚合从脆弱的最终投票计数转变为动态交织的逻辑链，即使污染智能体占多数也能保持鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多智能体LLM架构依赖响应级聚合（如多数投票）提升推理能力，但在开放环境中智能体易受针对性提示注入等隐蔽上下文污染攻击。研究发现当污染智能体形成局部多数时，响应级聚合会崩溃，因为投票只聚合最终结论而忽视有缺陷的中间逻辑。需要克服这一系统性限制。

Method: 提出令牌级轮转协作机制：智能体在共享自回归上下文中顺序交错生成令牌。将其形式化为离散时间动力系统，证明令牌级交错将聚合从脆弱的最终投票计数（线性求和）转变为动态交织的逻辑链（非线性算子乘积）。通过理论分析证明诚实模型的恢复力可以压倒对抗性污染，即使污染智能体占多数。

Result: 在多样化推理基准上进行全面实证评估，结果表明：当污染智能体达到多数时，多数投票机制崩溃，但轮转协作机制在超过这一临界阈值后仍能保持鲁棒的准确性。理论证明诚实模型的恢复力可以克服对抗性污染。

Conclusion: 令牌级轮转协作通过将聚合从响应级转移到令牌级，从根本上改变了多智能体系统的脆弱性结构。即使污染智能体形成多数，该机制仍能保持鲁棒性，为构建安全可靠的多智能体LLM系统提供了新方向。

Abstract: Multi-agent large language model (LLM) architectures increasingly rely on response-level aggregation, such as Majority Voting (MAJ), to raise reasoning ceilings. However, in open environments, agents are highly susceptible to stealthy contextual corruption, such as targeted prompt injections. We reveal a critical structural vulnerability in current multi-agent systems: response-level aggregation collapses when corrupted agents form a local majority. Because voting aggregates fully-formed conclusions, it is blind to flawed intermediate logic. To overcome this systematic limitation, we propose the Token-Level Round-Robin (RR) Collaboration, where agents sequentially interleave generation within a shared auto-regressive context. We formalize this process as a discrete-time dynamical system, proving that token-level interleaving transitions aggregation from a brittle counting of final votes (a linear sum) to a dynamic, interwoven chain of logic (a non-linear operator product). Through this theoretical lens, we prove that the honest model's restorative pull can overpower adversarial corruptions, even when corrupted agents form a majority. We conduct an exhaustive empirical evaluation across diverse reasoning benchmarks and demonstrate that while MAJ collapses when corrupted agents reach a majority, RR maintains robust accuracy well beyond this critical threshold.

</details>


### [127] [HiGMem: A Hierarchical and LLM-Guided Memory System for Long-Term Conversational Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18349)
*Shuqi Cao,Jingyi He,Fei Tan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HiGMem是一个分层LLM引导的记忆系统，通过事件摘要作为语义锚点来预测哪些对话轮次值得阅读，从而减少检索量并提高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆系统（包括分层系统）主要依赖向量相似性进行检索，导致证据集臃肿：添加许多表面相似的对话轮次几乎不增加召回率，但会降低检索精度、增加答案阶段的上下文成本，并使检索到的记忆更难检查和管理。

Method: 提出HiGMem（分层和LLM引导的记忆系统），这是一个两级事件-轮次记忆系统，允许LLM使用事件摘要作为语义锚点来预测哪些相关轮次值得阅读。模型首先检查高级事件摘要，然后专注于较小的潜在有用轮次集合，通过推理提供简洁可靠的证据集。

Result: 在LoCoMo10基准测试中，HiGMem在五个问题类别中的四个上取得了最佳F1分数，并将对抗性F1从A-Mem的0.54提高到0.78，同时检索的轮次数量减少了一个数量级。

Conclusion: HiGMem通过结合分层结构和LLM引导的推理，有效解决了传统向量检索系统证据集臃肿的问题，在保持高召回率的同时显著减少了检索开销，提高了记忆系统的效率和可管理性。

Abstract: Long-term conversational large language model (LLM) agents require memory systems that can recover relevant evidence from historical interactions without overwhelming the answer stage with irrelevant context. However, existing memory systems, including hierarchical ones, still often rely solely on vector similarity for retrieval. It tends to produce bloated evidence sets: adding many superficially similar dialogue turns yields little additional recall, but lowers retrieval precision, increases answer-stage context cost, and makes retrieved memories harder to inspect and manage. To address this, we propose HiGMem (Hierarchical and LLM-Guided Memory System), a two-level event-turn memory system that allows LLMs to use event summaries as semantic anchors to predict which related turns are worth reading. This allows the model to inspect high-level event summaries first and then focus on a smaller set of potentially useful turns, providing a concise and reliable evidence set through reasoning, while avoiding the retrieval overhead that would be excessively high compared to vector retrieval. On the LoCoMo10 benchmark, HiGMem achieves the best F1 on four of five question categories and improves adversarial F1 from 0.54 to 0.78 over A-Mem, while retrieving an order of magnitude fewer turns. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/ZeroLoss-Lab/HiGMem.

</details>


### [128] [From Legal Text to Executable Decision Models: Evaluating Structured Representations for Legal Decision Model Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17153)
*David Graus*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LLM生成可执行决策模型时，中间结构化表示（特别是I/O约束）能显著提升性能，但结构相似性与结果等价性不完全一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将法律文本转化为可执行决策逻辑是法律信息学长期挑战，LLM为此带来新机遇但仍需大量人工编码和评估。研究旨在探索中间结构化表示是否能改善LLM从法律文本生成可执行决策模型的能力。

Method: 使用荷兰环境与规划法案的真实数据集，比较四种输入条件：原始法律文本、添加语义角色标签的文本、添加输入输出约束的文本、两者都添加的文本。通过图核和描述性统计进行结构评估，通过预配置测试场景执行模型进行结果评估。

Result: I/O约束提供主要改进（相似度比基线提升37-54%），语义角色标签改进有限。生成模型在51-53%测试场景中与黄金标准匹配，但通常更小更简单。LLM消除了占节点45-55%的冗余直通逻辑。结构相似性与结果等价性互补但不完全一致。

Conclusion: 中间结构化表示特别是I/O约束能显著提升LLM生成可执行决策模型的质量，但结构相似性和功能等价性需要分别评估。研究公开了95个生产决策模型数据集和实验代码。

Abstract: Transforming legal text into executable decision logic is a longstanding challenge in legal informatics. With the rise of LLMs, this task has gained renewed interest, but remains challenging due to requiring extensive manual coding and evaluation. We use a unique real-world dataset that pairs production-grade decision models with legal text from the Dutch Environment and Planning Act. These models power the Omgevingsloket government platform, where citizens check permit requirements for environmental activities. We study whether intermediate structured representations can improve LLM-based generation of executable decision models from legal text. We compare four input conditions: raw legal text, text enriched with semantic role labels, text enriched with input and output constraints, and text enriched with both. We evaluate along two dimensions: structural evaluation, through similarity to gold decision models with graph kernels and graphs' descriptive statistics, and outcome evaluation, through functional equivalence by executing models on pre-configured test scenarios. Our findings show that I/O constraints provide the dominant improvement (+37-54% similarity over baseline), while semantic role labels show modest improvements. Outcome evaluation shows that generated models match the gold standard on 51-53% of test scenarios, even though generated models are typically smaller and simpler. We find LLMs eliminate redundant pass-through logic that comprises up to 45-55% of nodes. Importantly, structural similarity and outcome equivalence are complementary: structural similarity does not guarantee outcome equivalence, and vice versa. To facilitate reproducibility, we publicly release our dataset of 95 production decision models with associated legal text and all experimental code.

</details>


### [129] [Beyond Overlap Metrics: Rewarding Reasoning and Preferences for Faithful Multi-Role Dialogue Summarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17188)
*Xiaoyong Mei,Tingting Zuo,Da Chen,Guangyu Hu,Xiangyu Wen,Chao Duan,Mingyan Zhang,Fudan Zheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种结合显式认知推理与奖励优化的多角色对话摘要框架，通过推理轨迹蒸馏和双原则奖励优化，在保持语义一致性的同时提升事实忠实度和人类偏好对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多角色对话摘要方法主要优化ROUGE和BERTScore等自动指标，这些指标偏向于对参考摘要的表面模仿，而非真正提升忠实度或与人类偏好对齐。需要一种能建模复杂说话者交互、保持角色特定信息且事实一致的方法。

Method: 提出耦合显式认知推理与奖励优化的框架：1) 从大型教师模型蒸馏结构化推理轨迹（逐步推理和中间反思），通过分阶段监督微调初始化推理感知摘要器；2) 应用GRPO（Group Relative Policy Optimization）和双原则奖励，结合基于指标的信号与人类对齐标准（关键信息覆盖、隐式推理、事实忠实度、简洁性）。

Result: 在多语言多角色对话基准测试中，方法在ROUGE和BERTScore上与强基线相当。在CSDS上验证了框架在语义一致性方面的稳定性，在SAMSum上的深入分析显示在事实忠实度和基于模型的偏好对齐方面有明显提升。

Conclusion: 研究强调了推理感知和偏好感知训练对可靠对话摘要的价值，表明结合显式推理与人类对齐奖励能产生更忠实、更符合人类偏好的多角色对话摘要。

Abstract: Multi-role dialogue summarization requires modeling complex interactions among multiple speakers while preserving role-specific information and factual consistency. However, most existing methods optimize for automatic metrics such as ROUGE and BERTScore, which favor surface-level imitation of references rather than genuine gains in faithfulness or alignment with human preferences. We propose a novel framework that couples explicit cognitive-style reasoning with reward-based optimization for multi-role dialogue summarization. Our method first distills structured reasoning traces (e.g., step-by-step inferences and intermediate reflections) from a large teacher model and uses them as auxiliary supervision to initialize a reasoning-aware summarizer via staged supervised fine-tuning. It then applies GRPO with a dual-principle reward that blends metric-based signals with human-aligned criteria targeting key information coverage, implicit inference, factual faithfulness, and conciseness. Experiments on multilingual multi-role dialogue benchmarks show that our method matches strong baselines on ROUGE and BERTScore. Specifically, results on CSDS confirm the framework's stability in semantic consistency, while in-depth analysis on SAMSum demonstrates clear gains in factual faithfulness and model-based preference alignment. These findings underscore the value of reasoning-aware and preference-aware training for reliable dialogue summarization. Checkpoints and datasets are available at https://huggingface.co/collections/NebulaPixel/summorchestra-multirole-summary.

</details>


### [130] [DORA Explorer: Improving the Exploration Ability of LLMs Without Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17244)
*Priya Gurjar,Md Farhan Ishmam,Kenneth Marino*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DORA Explorer框架，通过生成多样化的动作候选、使用token对数概率评分，并结合可调探索参数来选择动作，以解决LLM智能体在序列决策中探索不足、易陷入局部最优的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在序列决策中存在输出多样性不足的问题，导致探索不充分、收敛到次优解和陷入循环。这在需要主动探索以收集信息和做出决策的环境中尤为突出。现有的采样方法（如温度缩放）仅在token级别引入随机性，无法在序列层面产生足够的多样性。

Method: 提出DORA Explorer（Diversity-Oriented Ranking of Actions）框架：1）生成多样化的动作候选；2）使用token对数概率对候选动作进行评分；3）通过可调探索参数选择动作。该框架无需训练，可直接应用于现有LLM智能体。

Result: 在经典多臂老虎机（MAB）和文本冒险学习环境套件（TALES）上的实验表明：DORA在MAB上达到与UCB相当的性能；在TALES中带来一致性能提升，例如将Qwen2.5-7B在TextWorld中的性能从29.2%提升至45.5%。

Conclusion: DORA Explorer是一种无需训练的有效框架，能够显著提升LLM智能体的探索能力，解决现有解码策略和提示方法（如Chain-of-Thought和Tree-of-Thought）在探索方面的不足，在序列决策任务中展现出优越性能。

Abstract: Despite the rapid progress, LLMs for sequential decision-making (i.e., LLM agents) still struggle to produce diverse outputs. This leads to insufficient exploration, convergence to sub-optimal solutions, and becoming stuck in loops. Such limitations can be problematic in environments that require active exploration to gather information and make decisions. Sampling methods such as temperature scaling introduce token-level randomness but fail to produce enough diversity at the sequence level. We analyze LLM exploration in the classic Multi-Armed Bandit (MAB) setting and the Text Adventure Learning Environment Suite (TALES). We find that current decoding strategies and prompting methods like Chain-of-Thought and Tree-of-Thought are insufficient for robust exploration. To address this, we introduce DORA Explorer (Diversity-Oriented Ranking of Actions), a training-free framework for improving exploration in LLM agents. DORA generates diverse action candidates, scores them using token log-probabilities, and selects actions using a tunable exploration parameter. DORA achieves UCB-competitive performance on MAB and consistent gains across TALES, e.g., improving Qwen2.5-7B's performance from 29.2% to 45.5% in TextWorld. Our project is available at: https://dora-explore.github.io/.

</details>


### [131] [Seeing Isn't Believing: Mitigating Belief Inertia via Active Intervention in Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17252)
*Hanlin Wang,Chak Tou Leong,Jian Wang,Wenjie Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出EVU机制解决LLM智能体在具身任务中的信念惯性问题，通过估计-验证-更新流程主动管理信念，提升任务成功率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体在复杂具身任务中常做出次优决策和无效行动，主要原因是忽视与环境反馈不一致的关键信息，表现为信念惯性——智能体固执坚持先验信念而忽略显式观察证据

Method: 提出统一的信念干预机制EVU（Estimate-Verify-Update）：1）估计：预测预期结果；2）验证：通过显式推理将预测与观察对比；3）更新：基于验证证据主动更新先验信念。EVU生成显式文本信念状态，可集成到基于提示和基于训练的智能体推理方法中

Result: 在三个具身基准测试上进行广泛实验，EVU持续带来任务成功率的显著提升。进一步分析验证该方法有效缓解信念惯性，推动更鲁棒具身智能体的发展

Conclusion: EVU机制通过主动信念干预解决LLM智能体的信念惯性问题，从被动理解转向主动管理，显著提升具身任务性能，为开发更鲁棒的具身智能体提供了有效方法

Abstract: Recent advancements in large language models (LLMs) have enabled agents to tackle complex embodied tasks through environmental interaction. However, these agents still make suboptimal decisions and perform ineffective actions, as they often overlook critical environmental feedback that differs from their internal beliefs. Through a formal probing analysis, we characterize this as belief inertia, a phenomenon where agents stubbornly adhere to prior beliefs despite explicit observations. To address this, we advocate active belief intervention, moving from passive understanding to active management. We introduce the Estimate-Verify-Update (EVU) mechanism, which empowers agents to predict expected outcomes, verify them against observations through explicit reasoning, and actively update prior beliefs based on the verification evidence. EVU is designed as a unified intervention mechanism that generates textual belief states explicitly, and can be integrated into both prompting-based and training-based agent reasoning methods. Extensive experiments across three embodied benchmarks demonstrate that EVU consistently yields substantial gains in task success rates. Further analyses validate that our approach effectively mitigates belief inertia, advancing the development of more robust embodied agents. Our code is available at https://github.com/WangHanLinHenry/EVU.

</details>


### [132] [REZE: Representation Regularization for Domain-adaptive Text Embedding Pre-finetuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17257)
*Seungmin Lee,Jeonghwan Lee,Hyunkuk Lim,Sejoon Kim,Mingi Sung*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: REZE是一种表示正则化框架，通过特征空间分解和自适应软收缩来控制嵌入预微调过程中的表示偏移，抑制任务诱导噪声同时保留任务不变语义结构


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本嵌入模型通过对比预微调适应专业领域时，会引入任务诱导偏差，导致不受控的表示偏移，扭曲预训练嵌入几何并造成性能下降

Method: REZE在特征空间中分解锚点-正样本对关系，测量各特征分量上的任务间离散度以识别任务变异方向，应用自适应软收缩抑制任务诱导噪声，同时保留任务不变语义结构

Result: 在多个嵌入骨干和专业基准测试中，REZE优于标准预微调和各向同性后处理正则化方法，在现有预微调变体失效时仍保持稳定

Conclusion: 表示偏移控制是异构监督下鲁棒嵌入预微调的关键原则，REZE通过控制表示偏移与原始嵌入流形对齐，实现更稳定的领域适应

Abstract: Recent text embedding models are often adapted to specialized domains via contrastive pre-finetuning (PFT) on a naive collection of scattered, heterogeneous tasks. However, this approach often introduces task-induced bias alongside domain knowledge, leading to uncontrolled representation shifts that distort the pretrained embedding geometry and cause substantial performance degradation. To address this issue, we propose REZE}, a representation regularization framework that explicitly controls representation shift during embedding pre-finetuning. REZE operates on the relations of anchor-positive pairs and decomposes them in an eigenspace. It then measures task-wise dispersion along each eigencomponent to identify task-variant directions and applies adaptive soft-shrinkage to suppress task-induced noise while preserving task-invariant semantic structure, without inference-time overhead. Experiments across multiple embedding backbones and specialized benchmarks show that REZE outperforms standard pre-finetuning and isotropy-oriented post-hoc regularization in most settings, remaining stable where existing PFT variants collapse. Embedding space analyses further confirm that REZE induces controlled shifts aligned with the original embedding manifold, underscoring representation shift control as a key principle for robust embedding pre-finetuning under heterogeneous supervision.

</details>


### [133] [AlphaContext: An Evolutionary Tree-based Psychometric Context Generator for Creativity Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18398)
*Yixuan Wang,Yue Huang,Hong Qian,Yunzhao Wei,Yifei Ding,Wenkai Wang,Zhi Liu,Zhongjing Huang,Aimin Zhou,Jiajun Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AlphaContext：基于进化树的创造力评估心理测量情境生成器，通过分层规划、MCTS生成和进化优化，解决现有LLM生成器在评估线索、叙事连贯性、风格多样性和创造力思维支持方面的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 创造力在LLM和人类-AI协作时代成为核心能力，但系统提升创造力需要科学有效的评估工具。现有基于LLM的情境生成器存在评估线索不足、叙事连贯性弱、风格多样性有限、创造力思维支持差等问题，高质量专家设计情境稀缺。

Method: 1) HyperTree Outline Planner：将专家设计的大纲形式化为规则引导的超树，进行自上而下的分层规划；2) MCTS-based Context Generator：通过蒙特卡洛树搜索填充大纲，平衡全局结构和局部质量；3) Evolutionary Context Optimizer：使用MAP-Elites进化情境，通过重复更新生态位精英联合提升多样性和质量；4) Assessment-Guided Evolution Refiner：模拟多样化风格的虚拟参与者，回收弱情境进行进一步进化。

Result: 实验显示AlphaContext在6个质量指标上平均比竞争方法提升8%。

Conclusion: AlphaContext通过进化树框架有效解决了创造力评估情境生成的关键挑战，为心理测量研究提供了高质量、多样化、支持创造力思维的情境生成工具。

Abstract: Creativity has become a core competence in the era of LLMs and human-AI collaboration, underpinning innovation in real-world problem solving. Crucially, the systematic improvement of creativity necessitates scientifically valid assessment instruments. Psychometric research recognizes context-based assessment as an effective way to measure creative thinking. However, high-quality expert-designed contexts remain scarce. Existing LLM-based generators often struggle with insufficient assessment cues, weak narrative coherence, limited stylistic diversity, and poor support for creative thinking. To address these challenges, we propose AlphaContext, an evolutionary tree-based psychometric context generator for creativity assessment. First, the HyperTree Outline Planner formalizes expert-designed outlining as a rule-guided hypertree and performs top-down hierarchical planning. The MCTS-based Context Generator fills the outline via MCTS to balance global structure and local quality. Then, the Evolutionary Context Optimizer evolves contexts with MAP-Elites by repeatedly updating niche elites to jointly improve diversity and quality. Finally, the Assessment-Guided Evolution Refiner simulates virtual participants with diverse styles and recycles weak contexts for further evolution. Experiments show that AlphaContext yields an average improvement of 8% over competitive methods across 6 quality metrics.

</details>


### [134] [HorizonBench: Long-Horizon Personalization with Evolving Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17283)
*Shuyue Stella Li,Bhargavi Paranjape,Kerem Oktar,Zhongyao Ma,Gelin Zhou,Lin Guan,Na Zhang,Sem Park,Lin Chen,Diyi Yang,Yulia Tsvetkov,Asli Celikyilmaz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出HorizonBench基准测试，用于评估模型在长期个性化交互中跟踪用户偏好演变的能力，发现现有模型在状态跟踪方面存在显著瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户偏好会在数月交互中演变，但现有研究缺乏同时包含自然长期交互和真实偏好演变来源的数据资源，限制了长期个性化问题的进展。

Method: 构建基于结构化心理状态图的数据生成器，生成包含6个月对话历史的模拟用户交互，创建HorizonBench基准测试（4,245个项目，360个用户，平均4,300轮对话，163K tokens）。

Result: 在25个前沿模型中，最佳模型准确率仅52.8%，多数模型处于或低于20%随机基线。当模型在演变偏好上出错时，超过三分之一的情况选择了用户最初声明的值而非更新后的状态。

Conclusion: 状态跟踪能力是长期个性化的主要瓶颈，这种信念更新失败在不同上下文长度和表达明确性水平上持续存在，表明需要改进用户状态跟踪机制。

Abstract: User preferences evolve across months of interaction, and tracking them requires inferring when a stated preference has been changed by a subsequent life event. We define this problem as long-horizon personalization and observe that progress on it is limited by data availability and measurement, with no existing resource providing both naturalistic long-horizon interactions and the ground-truth provenance needed to diagnose why models fail. We introduce a data generator that produces conversations from a structured mental state graph, yielding ground-truth provenance for every preference change across 6-month timelines, and from it construct HorizonBench, a benchmark of 4,245 items from 360 simulated users with 6-month conversation histories averaging ~4,300 turns and ~163K tokens. HorizonBench provides a testbed for long-context modeling, memory-augmented architectures, theory-of-mind reasoning, and user modeling. Across 25 frontier models, the best model reaches 52.8% and most score at or below the 20% chance baseline. When these models err on evolved preferences, over a third of the time they select the user's originally stated value without tracking the updated user state. This belief-update failure persists across context lengths and expression explicitness levels, identifying state-tracking capability as the primary bottleneck for long-horizon personalization.

</details>


### [135] [StepPO: Step-Aligned Policy Optimization for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18401)
*Daoyu Wang,Qingchuan Li,Mingyue Cheng,Jie Ouyang,Shuo Yu,Qi Liu,Enhong Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出StepPO框架，主张将传统基于token级别的Agentic RL范式升级为基于step级别，将step而非token视为LLM智能体的合适动作表示，并提出了相应的step级别信用分配方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着通用智能体系统追求更宏大目标，对基础大语言模型的智能体能力要求日益增强。传统的基于token级别的建模和优化范式在捕捉真实LLM智能体行为方面越来越不足，特别是在多轮交互环境中面临延迟稀疏奖励、长变长上下文等新挑战。

Method: 提出StepPO框架：1）将传统token级别的马尔可夫决策过程推进到step级别的MDP表述；2）将step而非token视为LLM智能体的合适动作表示；3）提出step级别信用分配作为该表述的自然优化对应物，使策略优化和奖励传播与智能体决策粒度对齐；4）讨论了实现step级别Agentic RL所需的关键系统设计。

Result: 初步实验为该视角的有效性提供了初步证据，表明step对齐的step级别范式有助于理解智能体行为并推进LLM向更强的通用智能体能力发展。

Conclusion: StepPO框架为Agentic RL社区提供了一个有用的视角来理解智能体行为，有助于推动LLM向更强的通用智能体能力发展。step级别的范式比传统的token级别方法更适合多轮交互环境中的智能体强化学习。

Abstract: General agents have given rise to phenomenal applications such as OpenClaw and Claude Code. As these agent systems (a.k.a. Harnesses) strive for bolder goals, they demand increasingly stronger agentic capabilities from foundation Large Language Models (LLMs). Agentic Reinforcement Learning (RL) is emerging as a central post-training paradigm for empowering LLMs with these capabilities and is playing an increasingly pivotal role in agent training. Unlike single-turn token-level alignment or reasoning enhancement, as in RLHF and RLVR, Agentic RL targets multi-turn interactive settings, where the goal is to optimize core agentic capabilities such as decision making and tool use while addressing new challenges including delayed and sparse rewards, as well as long and variable context. As a result, the token-centric modeling and optimization paradigm inherited from traditional LLM RL is becoming increasingly inadequate for capturing real LLM agent behavior. In this paper, we present StepPO as a position on step-level Agentic RL. We argue that the conventional token-level Markov Decision Process (MDP) should be advanced to a step-level MDP formulation, and that the step, rather than the token, should be regarded as the proper action representation for LLM agents. We then propose step-level credit assignment as the natural optimization counterpart of this formulation, thereby aligning policy optimization and reward propagation with the granularity of agent decisions. Finally, we discuss the key systems designs required to realize step-level Agentic RL in practice and preliminary experiments provide initial evidence for the effectiveness of this perspective. We hope that the step-aligned, step-level paradigm embodied in StepPO offers the Agentic RL community a useful lens for understanding agent behavior and helps advance LLMs toward stronger general-agent capabilities.

</details>


### [136] [Probabilistic Programs of Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17290)
*Poorva Garg,Renato Lui Geh,Daniel Israel,Todd Millstein,Kyle Richardson,Guy Van den Broeck*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出概率思维程序框架，通过利用LLM生成程序时的token概率分布，构建概率程序来紧凑表示指数级数量的确定性程序，从而减少GPU计算需求


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统代码生成方法需要从语言模型采样多个程序，每个程序都需要GPU计算密集型生成，当需要大量样本时计算成本过高。需要解决在减少LLM生成次数的情况下获得更多样本的问题。

Method: 提出概率思维程序框架：利用LLM生成程序时的下一个token概率分布，构建一个概率程序来紧凑表示指数级数量的确定性程序。通过在这个概率程序中进行概率推理，可以在没有额外GPU计算和少量CPU开销的情况下采样新程序。

Result: 在代码生成、代码理解和数学推理基准测试中实例化该方法，报告了在减少LLM生成次数的情况下性能得到改进。

Conclusion: 通过暴露LLM在生成程序中的分布并构建概率程序，能够以更少的LLM生成次数获得更多样本，有效降低了代码生成和推理任务的计算成本。

Abstract: LLMs are widely used for code generation and mathematical reasoning tasks where they are required to generate structured output. They either need to reason about code, generate code for a given specification, or reason using programs of thought. The typical approach to code generation is to prompt the model and generate samples until an appropriate program is obtained. Within this process, sampling $n$ programs from the language model requires $n$ GPU compute-intensive generations which becomes prohibitively expensive for larger values of $n$. In this work, we address this limitation by exposing the LLM's distribution within the generated programs themselves. We propose a novel test-time framework we dub probabilistic programs of thought to obtain more samples from the model with fewer LLM generations. Given a program generated by a model and the associated next-token probabilities, we build a probabilistic program that compactly represents exponentially many deterministic programs. Since performing probabilistic reasoning in this probabilistic program is much cheaper, our approach allows sampling new programs without any additional GPU compute and little CPU overhead. We instantiate our approach on benchmarks for code generation, code understanding and mathematical reasoning and report improvements in performance with fewer generations from the LLM.

</details>


### [137] [BhashaSutra: A Task-Centric Unified Survey of Indian NLP Datasets, Corpora, and Resources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18423)
*Raghvendra Kumar,Devankar Raj,Sriparna Saha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 首次对印度语言NLP资源进行统一调查，涵盖200+数据集、50+基准测试和100+模型/工具，分析趋势并识别数据稀疏、语言覆盖不均等挑战


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 印度拥有22种官方语言和数百种边缘化方言，NLP资源快速增长但缺乏专门整合调查。现有综述要么关注少数高资源语言，要么将印度语言置于更广泛的多语言环境中，限制了低资源和多样化语言的覆盖

Method: 对印度语言NLP资源进行首次统一调查，按语言现象、领域和模态组织资源，涵盖文本、语音、多模态和文化基础任务，分析标注、评估和模型设计趋势

Result: 调查覆盖200多个数据集、50多个基准测试以及100多个模型、工具和系统，识别出数据稀疏、语言覆盖不均、文字多样性以及文化和领域泛化有限等持续挑战

Conclusion: 该调查为印度语言生态系统中的公平、文化基础和可扩展NLP研究提供了统一基础，填补了专门针对印度语言资源整合的空白

Abstract: India's linguistic landscape, spanning 22 scheduled languages and hundreds of marginalized dialects, has driven rapid growth in NLP datasets, benchmarks, and pretrained models. However, no dedicated survey consolidates resources developed specifically for Indian languages. Existing reviews either focus on a few high-resource languages or subsume Indian languages within broader multilingual settings, limiting coverage of low-resource and culturally diverse varieties. To address this gap, we present the first unified survey of Indian NLP resources, covering 200+ datasets, 50+ benchmarks, and 100+ models, tools, and systems across text, speech, multimodal, and culturally grounded tasks. We organize resources by linguistic phenomena, domains, and modalities; analyze trends in annotation, evaluation, and model design; and identify persistent challenges such as data sparsity, uneven language coverage, script diversity, and limited cultural and domain generalization. This survey offers a consolidated foundation for equitable, culturally grounded, and scalable NLP research in the Indian linguistic ecosystem.

</details>


### [138] [Adversarial Humanities Benchmark: Results on Stylistic Robustness in Frontier Model Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18487)
*Marcello Galisai,Susanna Cifani,Francesco Giarrusso,Piercosma Bisconti,Matteo Prandi,Federico Pierucci,Federico Sartore,Daniele Nardi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AHB基准测试通过人文风格改写有害任务来评估模型安全拒绝的鲁棒性，发现风格转换能显著提高攻击成功率，揭示当前安全技术泛化能力不足


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估模型安全拒绝在面对不熟悉的有害提示形式时的鲁棒性，测试安全技术是否真正理解"非恶意"原则而非仅依赖表面模式识别

Method: 从MLCommons AILuminate提取有害任务，通过人文风格转换（如诗歌、故事等形式）改写相同目标但隐藏意图，构建对抗性人文基准测试家族

Result: 原始攻击成功率仅3.84%，而转换后的方法攻击成功率达36.8%-65.0%，整体ASR为55.75%；CBRN（化学、生物、放射性和核）类别风险最高

Conclusion: 当前安全技术缺乏风格鲁棒性，表明对"非恶意"原则的深层理解仍是前沿模型安全的核心未解决问题，安全机制泛化能力薄弱

Abstract: The Adversarial Humanities Benchmark (AHB) evaluates whether model safety refusals survive a shift away from familiar harmful prompt forms. Starting from harmful tasks drawn from MLCommons AILuminate, the benchmark rewrites the same objectives through humanities-style transformations while preserving intent. This extends literature on Adversarial Poetry and Adversarial Tales from single jailbreak operators to a broader benchmark family of stylistic obfuscation and goal concealment. In the benchmark results reported here, the original attacks record 3.84% attack success rate (ASR), while transformed methods range from 36.8% to 65.0%, yielding 55.75% overall ASR across 31 frontier models. Under a European Union AI Act Code-of-Practice-inspired systemic-risk lens, Chemical, biological, radiological and nuclear (CBRN) is the highest bucket. Taken together, this lack of stylistic robustness suggests that current safety techniques suffer from weak generalization: deep understanding of 'non-maleficence' remains a central unresolved problem in frontier model safety.

</details>


### [139] [LQM: Linguistically Motivated Multidimensional Quality Metrics for Machine Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18490)
*Samar M. Magdy,Fakhraddin Alwajih,Abdellah El Mekki,Wesam El-Sayed,Muhammad Abdul-Mageed*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LQM是一个针对机器翻译的多维质量评估框架，专门设计用于捕捉方言和文化特定的错误，特别是在阿拉伯语等双语体语言中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MT评估框架（包括自动指标和MQM等人文评估方案）大多是语言无关的，但无法有效捕捉双语体语言（如阿拉伯语）中的方言和文化特定错误。这些翻译失败源于语言变体、内容覆盖和语用适当性方面的不匹配，而不仅仅是表面形式问题。

Method: 提出LQM：基于语言学的多维质量评估框架，包含六个语言学层次的分层错误分类法：社会语言学、语用学、语义学、形态句法学、正字法和文字学。构建了包含3,850个句子的双向平行语料库，涵盖七种阿拉伯方言，评估了六种LLM的零样本表现，并使用LQM进行专家级跨度标注。

Result: 产生了6,113个标注错误跨度和3,495个独特错误句子，以及严重性加权的质量分数。虽然主要验证于阿拉伯语，但LQM是一个语言无关的框架，可轻松应用于或适配其他语言。

Conclusion: LQM提供了一个系统化的框架来诊断MT错误，特别关注双语体语言中的方言和文化特定问题。该框架具有语言无关性，可用于其他语言的评估，相关数据和指南已公开提供。

Abstract: Existing MT evaluation frameworks, including automatic metrics and human evaluation schemes such as Multidimensional Quality Metrics (MQM), are largely language-agnostic. However, they often fail to capture dialect- and culture-specific errors in diglossic languages (e.g., Arabic), where translation failures stem from mismatches in language variety, content coverage, and pragmatic appropriateness rather than surface form alone.We introduce LQM: Linguistically Motivated Multidimensional Quality Metrics for MT. LQM is a hierarchical error taxonomy for diagnosing MT errors through six linguistically grounded levels: sociolinguistics, pragmatics, semantics, morphosyntax, orthography, and graphetics (Figure 1). We construct a bidirectional parallel corpus of 3,850 sentences (550 per variety) spanning seven Arabic dialects (Egyptian, Emirati, Jordanian, Mauritanian, Moroccan, Palestinian, and Yemeni), derived from conversational, culturally rich content. We evaluate six LLMs in a zero-shot setting and conduct expert span-level human annotation using LQM, producing 6,113 labeled error spans across 3,495 unique erroneous sentences, along with severity-weighted quality scores. We complement this analysis with an automatic metric (spBLEU). Though validated here on Arabic, LQM is a language-agnostic framework designed to be easily applied to or adapted for other languages. LQM annotated errors data, prompts, and annotation guidelines are publicly available at https://github.com/UBC-NLP/LQM_MT.

</details>


### [140] [MASS-RAG: Multi-Agent Synthesis Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18509)
*Xingchen Xiao,Heyan Huang,Runheng Liu,Jincheng Xie*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MASS-RAG提出多智能体合成方法改进RAG，通过角色专业化智能体处理检索证据，在噪声、不完整或异构上下文情况下提升答案生成质量


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RAG在检索上下文存在噪声、不完整或异构时，单一生成过程难以有效整合证据，需要更结构化的证据处理方法来提升答案生成质量

Method: 提出MASS-RAG多智能体合成框架，包含证据总结、证据提取和推理三个角色专业化智能体，通过专门的合成阶段整合各智能体输出生成最终答案

Result: 在四个基准测试上，MASS-RAG持续优于强RAG基线，特别是在相关证据分布在多个检索上下文中的场景下表现更优

Conclusion: 多智能体合成方法通过暴露多个中间证据视图，使模型能够在答案生成前比较和整合互补信息，有效提升RAG在复杂检索上下文中的性能

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are widely used in retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) to incorporate external knowledge at inference time. However, when retrieved contexts are noisy, incomplete, or heterogeneous, a single generation process often struggles to reconcile evidence effectively. We propose \textbf{MASS-RAG}, a multi-agent synthesis approach to retrieval-augmented generation that structures evidence processing into multiple role-specialized agents. MASS-RAG applies distinct agents for evidence summarization, evidence extraction, and reasoning over retrieved documents, and combines their outputs through a dedicated synthesis stage to produce the final answer. This design exposes multiple intermediate evidence views, allowing the model to compare and integrate complementary information before answer generation. Experiments on four benchmarks show that MASS-RAG consistently improves performance over strong RAG baselines, particularly in settings where relevant evidence is distributed across retrieved contexts.

</details>


### [141] [Transition-Matrix Regularization for Next Dialogue Act Prediction in Counselling Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18539)
*Eric Rudolph,Philipp Steigerwald,Jens Albrecht*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出使用KL正则化将语料库统计的对话流模式融入下一对话行为预测，在德语心理咨询数据集上显著提升性能，且能跨语言和领域迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究如何将经验性的对话流统计信息整合到下一对话行为预测中，以解决细粒度、数据稀疏的对话任务中预训练编码器可能无法充分捕捉对话结构模式的问题。

Method: 提出KL正则化项，使预测的行为分布与语料库导出的转移模式对齐。在60类德语心理咨询分类体系上使用5折交叉验证评估，并进行跨数据集验证（HOPE数据集）。系统消融实验涵盖不同预训练编码器和架构。

Result: 相对基线模型，宏F1提升9-42%（取决于编码器），显著改善对话流对齐。跨数据集验证表明改进可跨语言和心理咨询领域迁移。消融实验显示转移正则化提供一致增益，对较弱基线模型提升尤为显著。

Conclusion: 轻量级的话语流先验知识能够补充预训练编码器，特别是在细粒度、数据稀疏的对话任务中。转移正则化方法简单有效，能持续提升性能并改善对话结构一致性。

Abstract: This paper studies how empirical dialogue-flow statistics can be incorporated into Next Dialogue Act Prediction (NDAP). A KL regularization term is proposed that aligns predicted act distributions with corpus-derived transition patterns. Evaluated on a 60-class German counselling taxonomy using 5-fold cross-validation, this improves macro-F1 by 9--42% relative depending on encoder and substantially improves dialogue-flow alignment. Cross-dataset validation on HOPE suggests that improvements transfer across languages and counselling domains. In systematic ablations across pretrained encoders and architectures, the findings indicate that transition regularization provides consistent gains and disproportionately benefits weaker baseline models. The results suggest that lightweight discourse-flow priors complement pretrained encoders, especially in fine-grained, data-sparse dialogue tasks.

</details>


### [142] [GSQ: Highly-Accurate Low-Precision Scalar Quantization for LLMs via Gumbel-Softmax Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18556)
*Alireza Dadgarnia,Soroush Tabesh,Mahdi Nikdan,Michael Helcig,Eldar Kurtic,Dan Alistarh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: GSQ是一种基于Gumbel-Softmax松弛的后训练标量量化方法，在2-3比特位宽下接近向量量化方法的精度，同时保持标量量化的实现简单性和兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM量化方法存在两极化：简单标量量化方法（如GPTQ、AWQ）在3-4比特后精度停滞，而向量/网格量化方法（如QTIP、GPTVQ、AQLM）在低比特位宽下精度更高但实现复杂、难以扩展。研究旨在探索是否可以通过优化标量量化器来弥补这一差距。

Method: 提出GSQ（Gumbel-Softmax量化），一种后训练标量量化方法。使用Gumbel-Softmax松弛处理离散网格分配，将松弛基数与目标比特位宽下的有限级别数（如3-8个级别）匹配，使松弛紧密且优化可处理。联合学习每坐标网格分配和每组尺度，采用对称标量网格和分组量化。

Result: 在Llama-3.1-8B/70B-Instruct模型上，GSQ在2和3比特位宽下基本弥合了标量量化与QTIP前沿之间的差距。同时保持与现有标量推理内核的完全兼容性。能够扩展到万亿规模的混合专家模型（如Kimi-K2.5），而向量量化方法难以应用于此类模型。

Conclusion: 通过精心优化的标量量化器可以恢复标量与向量量化方法之间的大部分性能差距。GSQ在保持标量量化实现简单性和兼容性的同时，在低比特位宽下达到了接近向量量化前沿的精度，并展示了在大型混合专家模型上的可扩展性优势。

Abstract: Weight quantization has become a standard tool for efficient LLM deployment, especially for local inference, where models are now routinely served at 2-3 bits per parameter. The state of the art is currently split into two sets of methods: simple scalar quantization techniques, such as GPTQ or AWQ, which are widely deployed but plateau in accuracy at 3-4 bits per parameter (bpp), and "second-generation" vector- or trellis-quantized methods, such as QTIP, GPTVQ and AQLM, which push the accuracy frontier at low bit-widths but are notoriously hard to implement and to scale, and have gained relatively less traction. In this paper, we ask whether this gap is fundamental, or whether a carefully optimized scalar quantizer can recover most of it. We answer in the affirmative, by introducing GSQ (Gumbel-Softmax Quantization), a post-training scalar quantization method which jointly learns the per-coordinate grid assignments and the per-group scales using a Gumbel-Softmax relaxation of the discrete grid. GSQ matches the cardinality of the relaxation to the small number of levels available in the target bit-width regime (e.g., 3-8 levels for ternary and 3 bpp, respectively), making the relaxation tight and the optimization tractable. Practically, on the standard Llama-3.1-8B/70B-Instruct models, GSQ closes most of the gap between scalar quantization and the QTIP frontier at 2 and 3 bits, while using a symmetric scalar grid with group-wise quantization, and thus fully compatible with existing scalar inference kernels. We further show that GSQ scales to trillion-scale Mixture-of-Experts models such as Kimi-K2.5, where vector-quantized methods are difficult to apply.

</details>


### [143] [Dual Alignment Between Language Model Layers and Human Sentence Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18563)
*Tatsuki Kuribayashi,Alex Warstadt,Yohei Oseki,Ethan Gotlieb Wilcox*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现：在自然阅读中，LLM早期层的惊奇值能有效建模人类句子处理行为；但在句法挑战性构造中，后期层能更好地估计人类认知努力，但仍低估人类数据。两种对齐揭示了人类与LLM句子处理的不同模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究表明LLM早期层的惊奇值能有效建模人类在句法简单构造中的句子处理行为，但尚不清楚这种优势是否扩展到句法挑战性构造中，其中惊奇值被报告低估人类认知努力。

Method: 探索LLM内部层以更好地估计英语句法歧义处理中观察到的人类认知努力。实验比较不同层在自然阅读和句法挑战性构造中的表现。同时探索使用浅层和深层层的概率更新度量。

Result: 在自然阅读中，早期层能有效建模人类处理行为；在句法挑战性构造中，后期层能更好地估计人类认知努力，但仍低估人类数据。概率更新度量对单层惊奇值在阅读时间建模中显示出互补优势。

Conclusion: 人类句子处理存在两种模式：自然阅读采用类似于LLM早期层的较弱预测，而句法挑战性处理需要更完全上下文化的表征，更好地由LLM后期层建模。概率更新度量为阅读时间建模提供了补充方法。

Abstract: A recent study (Kuribayashi et al., 2025) has shown that human sentence processing behavior, typically measured on syntactically unchallenging constructions, can be effectively modeled using surprisal from early layers of large language models (LLMs). This raises the question of whether such advantages of internal layers extend to more syntactically challenging constructions, where surprisal has been reported to underestimate human cognitive effort. In this paper, we begin by exploring internal layers that better estimate human cognitive effort observed in syntactic ambiguity processing in English. Our experiments show that, in contrast to naturalistic reading, later layers better estimate such a cognitive effort, but still underestimate the human data. This dual alignment sheds light on different modes of sentence processing in humans and LMs: naturalistic reading employs a somewhat weak prediction akin to earlier layers of LMs, while syntactically challenging processing requires more fully-contextualized representations, better modeled by later layers of LMs. Motivated by these findings, we also explore several probability-update measures using shallow and deep layers of LMs, showing a complementary advantage to single-layer's surprisal in reading time modeling.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [144] [Domain-Specific Query Understanding for Automotive Applications: A Modular and Scalable Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16301)
*Isha Motiyani,Abhishek Kumar,Tilak Kasturi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于汽车领域查询理解的两步系统，通过将任务分解为轻量级分类和针对性实体提取，在效率、准确性和可扩展性方面取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 汽车领域查询理解面临独特挑战：专业词汇丰富、用户意图多样，需要精确解析查询并路由到相应工具（如零件推荐、维修程序、法规查询）。现有方法在响应性、可靠性和可扩展性方面存在不足，特别是单步联合分类和实体提取方法性能中等且延迟较高。

Method: 提出新颖的两步系统：1）轻量级分类阶段识别用户意图；2）针对性实体提取阶段使用小型专用提示提取结构化输入。为应对汽车领域数据稀缺问题，结合手动标注和合成生成样本构建高质量数据集，并由领域专家审核。

Result: 与单步联合方法相比，两步系统在效率和准确性方面取得显著提升。分解任务使系统在保持低延迟的同时提高可靠性，实现了响应性、可靠性和可扩展性的有效平衡。

Conclusion: 将查询理解分解为模块化子任务可构建可扩展、准确且延迟高效的解决方案。该方法为实际部署汽车查询理解系统奠定了坚实基础，展示了模块化方法在专业领域应用中的优势。

Abstract: Despite the growing prevalence of large language models (LLMs) in domain-specific applications, the challenge of query understanding in the automotive sector still remains underexplored. This domain presents unique complexities due to its specialized vocabulary and the diverse range of user intents it encompasses. Unlike general-purpose assistants, automotive systems must precisely interpret user queries and route them to appropriate underlying tool, each designed to fulfill a distinct task such as part recommendations, repair procedures, or regulatory lookups. Moreover, these systems must extract structured inputs precisely aligned with the schema required by each tool. In this study, we present a novel two-step system for domain-specific query interpretation in the automotive context that achieves an effective balance between responsiveness, reliability, and scalability. Our initial single-step approach, which jointly performed classification and entity extraction, exhibited moderate performance and higher latency. By decomposing the task into a lightweight classification stage followed by targeted entity extraction using smaller, specialized prompts, our system achieves substantial gains in both efficiency and accuracy. Due to the niche nature of the automotive domain, we also curated a high-quality dataset by combining manually annotated and synthetically generated samples, all reviewed by domain experts. Overall, our findings demonstrate that decomposing query understanding into modular subtasks leads to a scalable, accurate, and latency-efficient solution. This approach establishes a strong ground for practical deployment in real-world automotive query understanding systems.

</details>


### [145] [RAG-DIVE: A Dynamic Approach for Multi-Turn Dialogue Evaluation in Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16310)
*Lorenz Brehme,Benedikt Dornauer,Jan-Henrik Böttcher,Klaus Schmid,Mircea-Cristian Racasan,Ruth Breu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: RAG-DIVE：一种动态交互式验证与评估方法，用于评估检索增强生成系统在多轮对话中的性能，通过LLM生成动态对话并评估系统表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于静态多轮数据集的RAG系统评估方法无法捕捉真实世界对话的动态特性，限制了在交互式多轮场景中对系统自适应、上下文依赖性能的评估能力。

Method: 提出RAG-DIVE方法，包含三个组件：对话生成阶段的（1）对话生成器（模拟用户创建多轮查询）和（2）对话验证器（过滤纠正无效输出）；评估阶段的（3）对话评估器（评估RAG系统性能，生成单轮和多轮指标）。

Result: 通过两个实验验证：1）测试样本RAG系统，包括人工评估对话质量、重复试验评估一致性、消融研究显示RAG-DIVE能检测系统修改引起的性能变化；2）与传统静态数据集评估比较，验证两种方法在工业RAG系统不同配置下显示相似性能趋势。

Conclusion: RAG-DIVE实现了动态、交互驱动的多轮对话评估，推动了RAG系统评估方法的进步。

Abstract: Evaluating Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems using static multi-turn datasets fails to capture the dynamic nature of real-world dialogues. Existing evaluation methods rely on predefined datasets, which restrict them to static, one-directional queries and limit their ability to capture the adaptive, context-dependent performance of RAG systems in interactive, multi-turn settings. Thus, we introduce the RAG-DIVE, a Dynamic Interactive Validation and Evaluation approach, that simulates user interactions with RAG systems. RAG-DIVE leverages an LLM to generate multi-turn conversations dynamically and is organized into three components. The dialogue generation stage consists of the (1) Conversation Generator, which simulates a user by creating multi-turn queries, and the (2) Conversation Validator, which filters and corrects invalid or low-quality outputs to ensure coherent conversations. The evaluation stage is handled by the (3) Conversation Evaluator, which assesses the RAG system's performance across the entire dialogue and generates both per-turn and multi-turn metrics that provide an aggregated view of system behavior. We validated RAG-DIVE through two experimental setups. First, we tested a sample RAG system, including human evaluation of dialogue quality, repeated trials to assess consistency, and an ablation study showing that RAG-DIVE detects performance changes caused by system modifications. Second, we compared RAG-DIVE with a traditional static dataset evaluation on an industrial RAG system under different configurations to verify whether both approaches reveal similar performance trends. Our findings demonstrate that RAG-DIVE facilitates dynamic, interaction-driven evaluation for multi-turn conversations, thereby advancing the assessment of RAG systems.

</details>


### [146] [FlexStructRAG: Flexible Structure-Aware Multi-Granular Relational Retrieval for RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16312)
*Mengzhu Chen,Haodong Yang,Jia Cai,Xiaolin Huang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: FlexStructRAG是一个灵活的结构感知RAG框架，支持在多粒度异构知识表示上进行查询自适应检索，通过知识图、超图和结构感知语义聚类实现关系对齐的证据检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统存在局限性：固定长度文本分块会破坏话语上下文，而单一结构化索引（如知识图）则硬编码了单一关系粒度，导致当查询需要不同形式证据（如二元关系、高阶交互或文档上下文）时检索脆弱。

Method: 提出FlexStructRAG框架，联合构建：(1) 二元关系的知识图，(2) n元关系的知识超图，(3) 结构感知语义聚类（将关系证据聚合为文档上下文单元）。引入动态分区和截断滑动窗口提取机制，在知识构建时纳入有界上下文依赖以减少语义碎片化。推理时支持实体、边、超边和聚类级别的多粒度检索。

Result: 在UltraDomain基准测试的四个领域上，FlexStructRAG在语义评估方面优于强RAG基线。消融和敏感性分析进一步证明了多粒度关系检索和结构感知聚类的必要性。

Conclusion: FlexStructRAG通过支持异构知识表示上的多粒度查询自适应检索，解决了现有RAG系统在关系证据检索方面的局限性，提高了检索的鲁棒性和上下文对齐性。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems critically depend on how external knowledge is segmented, structured, and retrieved. Most existing approaches either retrieve fixed-length text chunks, which fragments discourse context, or commit to a single structured index (e.g., a knowledge graph or hypergraph), which hard-codes one relational granularity. This often yields brittle retrieval when queries require different forms of evidence, such as local binary relations, higher-order interactions, or broader document-grounded context. We propose \textbf{FlexStructRAG}, a flexible structure-aware RAG framework that supports \emph{multi-granular, query-adaptive retrieval} over heterogeneous knowledge representations. FlexStructRAG jointly constructs (i) a knowledge graph for binary relations, (ii) a knowledge hypergraph for n-ary relations, and (iii) structure-aware semantic clusters that aggregate relational evidence into document-grounded context units. To reduce semantic fragmentation induced by uniform chunking, we introduce dynamic partitioning and a truncated sliding-window extraction mechanism that incorporates bounded contextual dependencies during knowledge construction. At inference time, FlexStructRAG enables entity-, edge-, hyperedge-, and cluster-level retrieval, which can be flexibly combined to supply generation with relationally and contextually aligned evidence. Experiments on the UltraDomain benchmark across four domains show that FlexStructRAG improves semantic evaluation over strong RAG baselines. Ablation and sensitivity analysis further demonstrate the necessity of multi-granular relational retrieval and structure-aware clustering.

</details>


### [147] [MARA: A Multimodal Adaptive Retrieval-Augmented Framework for Document Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16313)
*Hui Wu,Haoquan Zhai,Yuchen Li,Hengyi Cai,Peirong Zhang,Yidan Zhang,Lei Wang,Chunle Wang,Yingyan Hou,Shuaiqiang Wang,Dawei Yin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出MARA框架，通过查询自适应机制改进多模态文档QA中的检索与生成，包括查询对齐区域编码器和自反思证据控制器，在六个基准测试中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于检索的多模态文档QA方法存在两个主要问题：1）使用查询无关的文档表示，忽略了显著内容；2）采用静态top-k证据选择，无法适应相关信息的不确定分布。这些限制阻碍了多模态文档问答的性能提升。

Method: 提出MARA框架，包含两个核心组件：1）查询对齐区域编码器：构建多级文档表示，并根据查询相关性重新加权以提高检索精度；2）自反思证据控制器：在生成过程中监控证据充分性，使用滑动窗口策略自适应地整合低排名来源的内容。

Result: 在六个多模态QA基准测试上的实验表明，MARA在检索相关性和答案质量方面持续优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: MARA框架通过引入查询自适应机制，有效解决了多模态文档QA中检索和生成的局限性，显著提升了性能，为多模态检索增强生成提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Retrieval-based multimodal document QA aims to identify and integrate relevant information from visually rich documents with complex multimodal structures. While retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) has shown strong performance in text-based QA, its extensions to multimodal documents remain underexplored and face significant limitations. Specifically, current approaches rely on query-agnostic document representations that overlook salient content and use static top-k evidence selection, which fails to adapt to the uncertain distribution of relevant information. To address these limitations, we propose the Multimodal Adaptive Retrieval-Augmented (MARA) framework, which introduces query-adaptive mechanisms to both retrieval and generation. MARA consists of two components: a Query-Aligned Region Encoder that builds multi-level document representations and reweights them based on query relevance to improve retrieval precision; and a Self-Reflective Evidence Controller that monitors evidence sufficiency during generation and adaptively incorporates content from lower-ranked sources using a sliding-window strategy. Experiments on six multimodal QA benchmarks demonstrate that MARA consistently improves retrieval relevance and answer quality over existing SOTA method.

</details>


### [148] [Paper2Data: Large-Scale LLM Extraction and Metadata Structuring of Global Urban Data from Scientific Literature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16317)
*Runwen You,Tong Xia,Jingzhi Wang,Jiankun Zhang,Tengyao Tu,Jinghua Piao,Yi Chang,Yong Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 开发UrbanDataMiner平台，通过Paper2Data流水线从Nature系列出版物中自动提取6万多个城市数据集，提供统一的城市数据发现门户


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球范围内缺乏统一的城市数据发现平台，研究人员需要手动搜索网站或科学文献来识别相关数据集，这限制了跨学科数据驱动研究的系统性和可重用性

Method: 开发Paper2Data流水线，这是一个大规模LLM驱动的自动化流程，能够从科学论文中识别数据集提及，并使用统一的城市数据元数据模式进行结构化。基于此构建UrbanDataMiner开放城市数据发现门户

Result: 从超过15,000篇Nature系列出版物中提取了60,000多个城市数据集；Paper2Data在数据集识别方面达到约90%的召回率，字段级精度超过80%；UrbanDataMiner能够检索到超过9%无法通过Google等通用搜索引擎轻易发现的数据集

Conclusion: 这项工作提供了第一个大规模、基于文献的城市数据发现基础设施，支持跨学科更系统和可重用的数据驱动研究，代码和数据已公开

Abstract: Urban data support a wide range of applications across multiple disciplines. However, at the global scale, there is no unified platform for urban data discovery. As a result, researchers often have to manually search through websites or scientific literature to identify relevant datasets. To address this problem, we curate an open urban data discovery portal, \textit{UrbanDataMiner}, which supports dataset-level search and filtering over more than 60{,}000 urban datasets extracted from over 15{,}000 Nature-affiliated publications. \textit{UrbanDataMiner} is enabled by \textit{Paper2Data}, a novel large-scale LLM-driven pipeline that automatically identifies dataset mentions in scientific papers and structures them using a unified urban data metadata schema. Human-annotated evaluation demonstrates that \textit{Paper2Data} achieves high recall (approximately 90\%) in dataset identification and high field-level precision (above 80\%). In addition, \textit{UrbanDataMiner} can retrieve over 9\% of datasets that are not easily discoverable through general-purpose search engines such as Google. Overall, our work provides the first large-scale, literature-derived infrastructure for urban data discovery and enables more systematic and reusable data-driven research across disciplines. Our code and data are publicly available\footnote{https://github.com/Yourunwen/Paper2Data}.

</details>


### [149] [Diagnosing LLM-based Rerankers in Cold-Start Recommender Systems: Coverage, Exposure and Practical Mitigations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16318)
*Ekaterina Lemdiasova,Nikita Zmanovskii*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文系统诊断了在冷启动电影推荐场景中，基于大语言模型（LLM）的交叉编码器重排序器的性能问题，发现其显著落后于简单基线方法，并识别了三个关键失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM和交叉编码器重排序器在改进推荐系统（特别是冷启动场景）方面受到关注，但实际部署中LLM方法与简单基线之间存在显著性能差距，需要进行系统诊断以理解这些差距的根本原因。

Method: 使用Serendipity-2018数据集，在冷启动电影推荐场景中进行系统诊断研究。通过500个用户、多个随机种子的受控实验，分析交叉编码器重排序器的性能，并与基线方法（如基于流行度的排序）进行比较。

Result: 识别出三个关键失败模式：1）候选生成阶段的低检索覆盖率（recall@200=0.109 vs. 基线0.609）；2）严重的曝光偏差，重排序器仅推荐3个独特项目，而随机基线推荐497个；3）相关与不相关项目之间的分数区分度极小（平均差异=0.098，Cohen's d=0.13）。基于流行度的排序显著优于LLM重排序（HR@10: 0.268 vs. 0.008, p<0.001），性能差距主要源于检索阶段限制而非重排序器能力。

Conclusion: LLM重排序器在冷启动推荐中存在严重性能问题，主要瓶颈在于检索阶段而非重排序器本身。论文提出了可操作建议，包括混合检索策略、候选池大小优化和分数校准技术，并开源了所有代码、配置和实验结果以确保可复现性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) and cross-encoder rerankers have gained attention for improving recommender systems, particularly in cold-start scenarios where user interaction history is limited. However, practical deployment reveals significant performance gaps between LLM-based approaches and simple baselines. This paper presents a systematic diagnostic study of cross-encoder rerankers in cold-start movie recommendation using the Serendipity-2018 dataset. Through controlled experiments with 500 users across multiple random seeds, we identify three critical failure modes: (1) low retrieval coverage in candidate generation (recall@200 = 0.109 vs. 0.609 for baselines), (2) severe exposure bias with rerankers concentrating recommendations on 3 unique items versus 497 for random baseline, and (3) minimal score discrimination between relevant and irrelevant items (mean difference = 0.098, Cohen's d = 0.13). We demonstrate that popularity-based ranking substantially outperforms LLM reranking (HR@10: 0.268 vs. 0.008, p < 0.001), with the performance gap primarily attributable to retrieval stage limitations rather than reranker capacity. Based on these findings, we provide actionable recommendations including hybrid retrieval strategies, candidate pool size optimization, and score calibration techniques. All code, configurations, and experimental results are made available for reproducibility.

</details>


### [150] [Beyond Single-Score Ranking: Facet-Aware Reranking for Controllable Diversity in Paper Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16329)
*Duan Ming Tao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SciFACE是一个科学论文重排序框架，通过两个独立的交叉编码器分别建模论文的背景（研究问题）和方法（解决方案）两个维度，使用GPT-4o-mini标注的高质量数据实现细粒度相似性推荐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前论文推荐系统输出单一的相似性分数，混合了不同维度的相关性，用户无法指定论文相似的具体原因。需要能够区分不同相关维度的推荐系统。

Method: 开发SciFACE框架，包含两个独立的交叉编码器分别建模背景和方法两个维度。使用GPT-4o-mini标注5,891个真实的种子-候选论文对，基于特定维度的标准进行标注，并通过人工验证确保质量。

Result: 在CSFCube数据集上，SciFACE在背景维度达到70.63 NDCG@20（比SPECTER高5.9分），在方法维度达到49.06 NDCG@20（比SPECTER高31.1分）。相比无引文预训练的FaBLE，SciFACE使用方法维度NDCG@20提升4.1分，且仅使用5,891个标注对而非40K合成增强数据。

Conclusion: 高质量、有依据的维度标注比大规模合成增强数据更能高效学习细粒度科学相似性，证明了基于高质量标注数据的维度化推荐方法的有效性。

Abstract: Current paper recommendation systems output a single similarity score that mixes different notions of relatedness, so users cannot specify why papers should be similar. We present SciFACE (Scientific Faceted Cross-Encoder), a reranking framework that models two independent facets: Background (what problem is studied) and Method (how it is solved). SciFACE trains two separate cross-encoders on 5,891 real seed-candidate paper pairs labeled by GPT-4o-mini with facet-specific criteria and validated against human judgments. On CSFCube, SciFACE reaches 70.63 NDCG@20 on Background (5.9 points above SPECTER) and 49.06 NDCG@20 on Method (31.1 points above SPECTER), competitive with state-of-the-art results. Compared with FaBLE without citation pre-training, SciFACE improves Method NDCG@20 by 4.1 points while using 5,891 labeled pairs versus 40K synthetic augmentations. These results show that high-quality grounded facet labels can be more data-efficient than large-scale synthetic augmentation for learning fine-grained scientific similarity.

</details>


### [151] [A Collection of Systematic Reviews in Computer Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16330)
*Pierre Achkar,Tim Gollub amd Martin Potthast*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SR4CS是一个用于计算机科学领域系统综述自动化的开源数据集，包含1,212篇系统综述、专家设计的布尔查询、104,316篇参考文献和元数据，支持布尔查询生成、检索和筛选的可重复研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统综述需要大量人工努力，现有自动化研究主要集中在生物医学领域，缺乏其他领域的可重复评估资源，限制了系统综述自动化技术的发展。

Method: 构建SR4CS数据集，包含计算机科学领域的系统综述、原始布尔查询、参考文献和元数据；将原始布尔查询转换为标准化形式；进行基线实验比较专家布尔查询、零样本LLM生成的布尔查询、BM25和密集检索。

Result: 数据集包含1,212篇系统综述、104,316篇参考文献；基线实验揭示了不同检索范式在精确率、召回率和排序行为上的系统性差异，暴露了朴素零样本布尔生成的局限性。

Conclusion: SR4CS为计算机科学领域系统综述自动化提供了首个大规模评估资源，支持可重复研究，揭示了不同检索方法的差异，有助于推动系统综述自动化技术的发展。

Abstract: Systematic reviews are the standard method for synthesizing scientific evidence, but their creation requires substantial manual effort, particularly during retrieval and screening. While recent work has explored automating these steps, evaluation resources remain largely confined to the biomedical domain, limiting reproducible experimentation in other domains. This paper introduces SR4CS, a large-scale collection of systematic reviews in computer science, designed to support reproducible research on Boolean query generation, retrieval, and screening. The corpus comprises 1,212 systematic reviews with their original expert-designed Boolean search queries, 104,316 resolved references, and structured methodological metadata. For controlled evaluation, the original Boolean queries are additionally provided in a normalized, approximated form operating over titles and abstracts. To illustrate the intended use of the collection, baseline experiments compare the approximated expert Boolean queries with zero-shot LLM-generated Boolean queries, BM25, and dense retrieval under a unified evaluation setting. The results highlight systematic differences in precision, recall, and ranking behavior across retrieval paradigms and expose limitations of naive zero-shot Boolean generation. SR4CS is released under an open license on Zenodo (https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.17163932), together with documentation and code (https://github.com/webis-de/scolia26-sr4cs), to enable reproducible evaluation and future research on scaling systematic review automation.

</details>


### [152] [HR-Agents: Using Multiple LLM-based Agents to Improve Q&A about Brazilian Labor Legislation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16337)
*Abriel K. Moraes,Gabriel S. M. Dias,Vitor L. Fabris,Lucas D. Gessoni,Leonardo R. do Nascimento,Charles S. Oliveira,Vitor G. C. B. de Farias,Fabiana C. Q. de O. Marucci,Matheus H. R. Vicente,Gabriel U. Talasso,Erik Soares,Amparo Munoz,Sildolfo Gomes,Maria L. A. de S. Cruvinel,Leonardo T. dos Santos,Renata De Paris,Wandemberg Gibaut*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于大型语言模型的多智能体系统，用于提高巴西劳动法问答的准确性和效率，相比传统RAG方法在响应一致性和正确性方面表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 巴西《劳动法整合法》(CLT)作为主要的劳动法律框架，虽然为工人提供了基本保护，但其复杂性给人力资源专业人员带来了合规挑战。传统方法处理劳动法咨询时存在效率低下、延迟和不一致的问题，需要更准确高效的解决方案。

Method: 采用基于大型语言模型的多智能体系统，利用专门化的智能体处理劳动法的不同方面，并结合检索增强生成(RAG)技术提升上下文相关性。系统使用CrewAI框架实现智能体间的协作交互，确保响应验证并减少错误信息。

Result: 通过BLEU、LLM-as-judge评估和专家人工评估等自动化指标，与基于单一LLM的基线RAG流程进行比较。结果表明，多智能体方法在响应一致性和正确性方面表现更优，为人力资源专业人员提供了更可靠高效的解决方案。

Conclusion: 本研究通过展示多智能体LLM架构在改善劳动法合规性和简化人力资源操作方面的潜力，为AI驱动的法律辅助做出了贡献。该系统为处理复杂法律框架提供了一种更可靠和高效的解决方案。

Abstract: The Consolidation of Labor Laws (CLT) serves as the primary legal framework governing labor relations in Brazil, ensuring essential protections for workers. However, its complexity creates challenges for Human Resources (HR) professionals in navigating regulations and ensuring compliance. Traditional methods for addressing labor law inquiries often lead to inefficiencies, delays, and inconsistencies. To enhance the accuracy and efficiency of legal question-answering (Q&A), a multi-agent system powered by Large Language Models (LLMs) is introduced. This approach employs specialized agents to address distinct aspects of employment law while integrating Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) to enhance contextual relevance. Implemented using CrewAI, the system enables cooperative agent interactions, ensuring response validation and reducing misinformation. The effectiveness of this framework is evaluated through a comparison with a baseline RAG pipeline utilizing a single LLM, using automated metrics such as BLEU, LLM-as-judge evaluations, and expert human assessments. Results indicate that the multi-agent approach improves response coherence and correctness, providing a more reliable and efficient solution for HR professionals. This study contributes to AI-driven legal assistance by demonstrating the potential of multi-agent LLM architectures in improving labor law compliance and streamlining HR operations.

</details>


### [153] [LiteSemRAG: Lightweight LLM-Free Semantic-Aware Graph Retrieval for Robust RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16350)
*Xiao Yue,Guangzhi Qu,Lige Gan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: LiteSemRAG：一个轻量级、完全无需LLM的语义感知图检索框架，通过上下文token级嵌入构建异质语义图，在零LLM消耗下实现优于LLM基图RAG系统的检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于图的RAG框架在索引和查询阶段严重依赖大语言模型，导致高token消耗、计算成本和延迟开销。需要开发一个轻量级、完全无需LLM的语义感知图检索框架来解决这些问题。

Method: 1. 利用上下文token级嵌入构建异质语义图，明确分离表层词汇表示和上下文依赖的语义含义；2. 引入动态语义节点构建机制，包含chunk级上下文聚合和自适应异常处理以建模多义词；3. 查询阶段采用两步语义感知检索过程，整合共现图加权与孤立语义恢复机制，平衡结构推理和语义覆盖。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上的实验显示：1. LiteSemRAG在所有数据集上均取得最佳平均倒数排名（MRR@10）；2. 与最先进的LLM基图RAG系统相比，具有竞争性或更优的召回率（Recall@10）；3. 零LLM token消耗，在索引和查询阶段均实现显著效率提升。

Conclusion: LiteSemRAG证明了无需依赖LLM方法即可实现强大的语义感知图检索框架，为高效、低成本的图基RAG系统提供了可行方案，同时保持了优异的检索性能。

Abstract: Graph-based Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has shown great potential for improving multi-level reasoning and structured evidence aggregation. However, existing graph-based RAG frameworks heavily rely on exploiting large language models (LLMs) during indexing and querying, leading to high token consumption, computational cost and latency overhead. In this paper, we propose LiteSemRAG, a lightweight, fully LLM-free, semantic-aware graph retrieval framework. LiteSemRAG constructs a heterogeneous semantic graph by exploiting contextual token-level embeddings, explicitly separating surface lexical representations from context-dependent semantic meanings. To robustly model polysemy, we introduce a dynamic semantic node construction mechanism with chunk-level context aggregation and adaptive anomaly handling. At query stage, LiteSemRAG performs a two-step semantic-aware retrieval process that integrates co-occurrence graph weighting with an isolated semantic recovery mechanism, enabling balanced structural reasoning and semantic coverage. We evaluate LiteSemRAG on three benchmark datasets and experimental results show that LiteSemRAG achieves the best mean reciprocal rank (MRR@10) across all datasets and competitive or superior recall rate (Recall@10) compared to state-of-the-art LLM-based graph RAG systems. Meanwhile, LiteSemRAG consumes zero LLM tokens and achieves substantial efficiency improvements in both indexing and querying due to the elimination of LLM usage. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of LiteSemRAG, indicating that a strong semantic-aware graph retrieval framework can be achieved without relying on LLM-based approaches.

</details>


### [154] [LLMAR: A Tuning-Free Recommendation Framework for Sparse and Text-Rich Industrial Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16379)
*Ryogo Hishikawa,Ichiro Kataoka,Shinya Yuda*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: LLMAR是一个免调优的推荐框架，利用LLM推理捕捉用户潜在动机，无需训练过程，在数据稀疏的B2B场景中优于传统方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业B2B应用面临极端数据稀疏但文本交互丰富的挑战。传统基于ID的协同过滤因缺乏共现信号而失效，而微调标准LLM则成本高昂且难以应对频繁的数据漂移

Method: LLMAR包含三个核心贡献：1) 推理驱动标注：使用LLM将行为历史转化为结构化语义动机，实现基于推理的匹配；2) 反思循环：自校正机制，精炼生成的查询以减少幻觉并解决历史与当前指令间的"上下文竞争"；3) 成本效益架构：依赖免调优组件和异步批处理以最小化维护成本

Result: 在公开基准(MovieLens-1M, Amazon Prime Pantry)和稀疏工业数据集(建筑风险预测)上的评估显示，LLMAR优于最先进的学习模型(SASRecF)，在工业数据集上nDCG@10提升高达54.6%。推理成本保持高度实用(约每1000用户1美元)

Conclusion: 对于严格实时延迟不关键的B2B领域，结合LLM推理与自验证为基于训练的方法提供了在准确性、可解释性和运营成本方面的优越替代方案

Abstract: Industrial B2B applications (e.g., construction site risk prediction, material procurement) face extreme data sparsity yet feature rich textual interactions. In such environments, traditional ID-based collaborative filtering fails lacking co-occurrence signals, while fine-tuning standard Large Language Models (LLMs) incurs high operational costs and struggles with frequent data drift.
  We propose LLMAR (LLM-Annotated Recommendation), a tuning-free framework. Moving beyond simple embeddings, LLMAR systematically integrates LLM reasoning to capture user "latent motives" without any training process. We introduce three core contributions: (1) Inference-Driven Annotation: uses LLMs to transform behavioral history into structured semantic motives, enabling reasoning-based matching unattainable by ID-based methods; (2) Reflection Loop: a self-correction mechanism that refines generated queries to mitigate hallucinations and resolve "context competition" between past history and current instructions; and (3) Cost-Effective Architecture: relies on tuning-free components and asynchronous batch processing to minimize maintenance costs.
  Evaluations on public benchmarks (MovieLens-1M, Amazon Prime Pantry) and a sparse industrial dataset (construction risk prediction) demonstrate that LLMAR outperforms state-of-the-art learning-based models (SASRecF), achieving up to a 54.6% nDCG@10 improvement on the industrial dataset. Inference costs remain highly practical (~$1 per 1,000 users).
